| Know what my downside is? No matter how much I might want to, I don't fucking make mistakes. Lo and behold! Our factory-reset ronin, hounded by every single Arasaka soldier in Night City for KIDNAPPING Saburo Arasaka's daughter. Kudos to him – I couldn't've fucked this plan up better myself. And after all this, he STILL wants you to drop by? I don't know who's more whacked – him or us. Just remember to knock four times. His head, preferably – against a fucking table. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

868 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 130 distinct direct resource identities; 37 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
